([swaruplab/operon](https://github.com/swaruplab/operon/tree/HEAD/protocols/multi-omics-integration-data-harmonization) · ★104 · 2 copies)
- **multi-omics-data-integration**: Use when when you have matched transcriptomics (RNA-seq read counts), intracellular metabolomics (LC-MS abundance data), and extracellular flux measurements (YSI bioanalyzer or similar) from multiple biological samples or cell lines, and you need to determine whether differences in metabolic enzyme. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/multi-omics-data-integration) · 3 copies)
- **multi-omics-data-integration**: Use when you have &gt;=2 molecular abundance matrices measured on the same individuals and need a predictive, pathway-level multi-omics model that ranks pathways and quantifies each omics layer's contribution. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/multi-omics-data-integration))
- **multi-omics-data-object-construction**: Use when you have paired sample-level copy-number/epi-DNA alteration calls and matched gene expression matrices and need to package them, with cis gene lists and sample/feature annotations, into the iEDGE object required by run_iEDGE. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/multi-omics-data-object-construction))
- **multi-omics-data-preprocessing**: Use when raw DepMap/CCLE omics feature tables (CNV, mutations, methylation, histone PTMs, RNA, miRNA, lncRNA, proteomics, phosphoproteomics) must be cleaned and normalized before training per-metabolite ML models. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/multi-omics-data-preprocessing))
- **multi-omics-data-zero-filtering**: Use when two or more omics abundance matrices must be harmonized before cross-omics correlation or network analysis, and raw matrices contain many zero (unmeasured) entries that would distort Spearman correlations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/multi-omics-data-zero-filtering))
- **multi-omics-database-access**: Workflow for retrieving public omics datasets, sequences, annotations, and literature-linked biological resources from repositories such as NCBI GEO/SRA, UniProt, Reactome, and PubMed. ([ai4nucleome/BioMaster](https://github.com/ai4nucleome/BioMaster/tree/HEAD/.opencode/bioskills/multi-omics-database-access) · ★125)
- **multi-omics-de-count-validation**: Use when processing concurrently generated untargeted metabolomic and lipidomic LC-MS/MS feature tables (e.g. from the Multi-ABLE method) through filtering, imputation, EigenMS normalization, and limma differential expression, then verifying reported DE feature counts at adjusted p &lt; 0.05. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/multi-omics-de-count-validation))
- **multi-omics-dimension-reduction**: Use when you have paired multi-omics count matrices (e.g. gene expression + protein ADT, or ATAC gene activity + mRNA) measured on the same cells/spots and need an integrated low-dimensional representation for clustering, with or without spatial coordinates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/multi-omics-dimension-reduction))
- **multi-omics-embedding-fusion**: Use when you have paired transcriptomic and proteomic single-cell or spatial multi-omics data in AnnData format with precomputed neighborhood graphs (adata.uns['nbrs_net']) and need a fused low-dimensional joint embedding per cell for downstream analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/multi-omics-embedding-fusion))
- **multi-omics-factor-analysis**: Use when two or more standardized omics matrices (e.g. top-10%-most-variable DNAm beta values and creatinine-normalized urine metabolite intensities) need to be integrated into shared latent factors without a priori labels. - **multi-omics-feature-importance-aggregation**: Use when per-metabolite model feature importances exist across multiple omics feature sets (e.g., 9 feature sets x 225 metabolites) and the goal is a global, cross-omics top-regulator ranking. - **multi-sample-cnv-call-merging**: Use when you have CNV calls from several related samples (e.g., trio or cohort .pytor files) and need to produce a unified joint call set with sample membership and per-sample genotypes rather than per-sample call lists. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/multi-sample-cnv-call-merging))
- **multi-sample-cohort-assessment**: Use when when you have MS1 mass spectrometry data from multiple samples (a cohort) acquired across an instrument run or batch, and you need to identify which samples deviate from cohort norms or which ion targets show anomalous behavior. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/multi-sample-cohort-assessment) · 4 copies)
- **multi-sample-consistency**: 7 樣本跨樣本 parallel benchmark + 一致性檢查模板。任何結論需跨 4+ 樣本驗證時觸發。產生 canonical 排序表、方向一致性統計、confidence uplift 計算。觸發：「cross-sample」「multi-sample」「7 樣本驗證」「跨樣本一致」「consistency check」「P4 generalize」、tier 升級到 ⭐3+ 前。SKIP WHEN 單樣本 pilot 階段（用 feature-layered-observation）、結論僅需 1-3 樣本驗證、純 binary build / commit、純 docs 寫作、未涉及跨樣本比較。 ([liaoyoyo/InterSubMod](https://github.com/liaoyoyo/InterSubMod/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/multi-sample-consistency) · ★1)
- **multi-sample-consistency**: 7 樣本跨樣本 parallel benchmark + 一致性檢查模板。任何結論需跨 4+ 樣本驗證時觸發。產生 canonical 排序表、方向一致性統計、confidence uplift 計算。觸發：「cross-sample」「multi-sample」「7 樣本驗證」「跨樣本一致」「consistency check」。 ([liaoyoyo/InterSubMod](https://github.com/liaoyoyo/InterSubMod/tree/HEAD/docs/archive/2026/05/20260509_codex_nested_skills_duplicate/nested_skills_duplicate/multi-sample-consistency) · ★1)
- **multi-sample-contig-pooling**: Use when building a species-level non-redundant viral contig catalog from multiple shotgun viral metagenomic samples, requiring per-sample assembly followed by cross-sample dereplication. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/multi-sample-contig-pooling))
- **multi-sample-coverage-profile-processing**: Use when assembling metagenomic short reads into contigs and you need sample-wise read coverage per contig as input features for a binner such as CH-Bin. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/multi-sample-coverage-profile-processing))
- **multi-sample-metabolomics-data-integration**: Use when you have two or more independently processed MemoMatrix objects (each generated from a separate sample set) and your analysis goal requires direct comparison of MS2 fingerprint profiles across those samples. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/multi-sample-metabolomics-data-integration) · 3 copies)
- **multi-sample-multi-condition-scrna-seq-analysis**: Use when scRNA-seq or snRNA-seq data span multiple biological samples and experimental groups (e.g., vehicle vs LPS-treated mice) and the goal is cell-subpopulation-level differential state inference rather than across-cell comparisons. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/multi-sample-multi-condition-scrna-seq-analysis))
- **multi-sample-outlier-detection-via-spectral-similarity**: Use when pre-processing a multi-sample MALDI MSI dataset and you need to automatically detect samples whose spectra deviate from the spectral similarity expected across samples before downstream analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/multi-sample-outlier-detection-via-spectral-similarity))
- **multi-sample-photo-z-comparison-analysis**: Use when benchmarking a photo-z method's catastrophic outlier rates (O(a): |zphot-zspec|&gt;0.10; O(b): |zphot-zspec|/(1+zspec)&gt;0.10) against published codes across simulated (PHAT0) and real (SDSS DR10) samples. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/multi-sample-photo-z-comparison-analysis))
- **multi-sample-preprocessing-benchmarking**: Use when evaluating a new SPLiT-seq preprocessing tool against the original barcode/UMI filtering baseline across five or more samples, and you need per-sample identified-read overlap and 96-97% consistency rates as validation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/multi-sample-preprocessing-benchmarking))
- **multi-sample-pseudotime-xde-testing**: Use when analyzing multi-sample scRNA-seq pseudotime trajectories to identify genes whose expression along pseudotime differs across a covariate (e.g. sex, disease severity) while adjusting for batch effects. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/multi-sample-pseudotime-xde-testing))
- **multi-sample-qc-report-aggregation**: Use when per-sample QC and methylation outputs (FastQC, SAMtools, Picard, MEDIPS/QSEA/MEDStrand quantifications) must be pooled across many samples, groups, or batches into a summarized report and region-queryable aggregated matrices. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/multi-sample-qc-report-aggregation))
- **multi-sample-scrna-seq-data-preparation**: Use when preparing multi-sample scRNA-seq expression, pseudotime, and sample-covariate data for differential pseudotime (XDE/TDE) testing, or when a Monocle2 trajectory must be retrofitted for covariate-associated differential expression tests. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/multi-sample-scrna-seq-data-preparation))
- **multi-sample-scrna-seq-differential-pseudotime-analysis**: Use when you have scRNA-seq data from multiple biological samples with covariates (e.g. sex, disease status) and a reconstructed pseudotime trajectory, and you need to test which genes or density patterns change along pseudotime in association with those covariates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/multi-sample-scrna-seq-differential-pseudotime-analysis))
- **multi-sample-scrnaseq-data-simulation**: Use when benchmarking differential state analysis methods on scRNA-seq data, or when ground-truth multi-sample multi-subpopulation counts with known DS genes/logFCs are needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/multi-sample-scrnaseq-data-simulation))
- **multi-sample-short-read-binning-workflow**: Use when reconstructing MAGs from dozens of short-read metagenomic samples (e.g., human gut, dog gut, ocean, soil projects with 82–129 samples) for which assembled contigs and sorted BAM files are available and joint binning is desired. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/multi-sample-short-read-binning-workflow))
- **multi-sample-srt-data-integration**: Use when two or more SRT sections (e.g. Visium DLPFC sections or seqFISH mouse embryo sections) across batches, individuals, or conditions must be jointly clustered while correcting batch effects. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/multi-sample-srt-data-integration))
- **multi-sample-trajectory-statistical-modeling**: Use when scRNA-seq data from multiple biological samples per condition need pseudotemporal trajectory comparison across covariates (e.g. mild vs moderate COVID-19), and naive single-sample pseudotime methods would ignore sample-to-sample variation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/multi-sample-trajectory-statistical-modeling))
- **multi-scale-coordinator**: Add a cross-scale consistency mechanism that aligns representations or decisions across scales without making coordination overhead the main contribution. ([OpenDFM/Xcientist](https://github.com/OpenDFM/Xcientist/tree/HEAD/src/agents/idea_agent/agent/skills/edit_operator_skills/multi-scale-coordinator) · ★72)
- **multi-scale-hypergraph-brain-connectivity**: Multi-scale hypergraph learning (MuHL) methodology for high-order brain connectivity analysis beyond pairwise GNNs. Accepted to ICML 2026. Use for: brain network analysis, neurodegenerative disease classification (Alzheimer's, Parkinson's), higher-order functional connectivity, hypergraph neural networks. ([hiyenwong/ai_collection](https://github.com/hiyenwong/ai_collection/tree/HEAD/collection/skills/neuroscience/multi-scale-hypergraph-brain-connectivity) · ★4 · 2 copies)
- **multi-scale-info-geometry-neural**: Multi-scale information geometry framework revealing the structure of mutual information in neural populations. A unique Riemannian representational geometry emerges from coarse-graining, extending Fisher information metric to capture encoding structure from fine to coarse stimulus distinctions. Use when researching neural population coding, information geometry, Fisher information in neuroscience, or neural representational geometry. Based on arXiv:2605.06304. ([hiyenwong/ai_collection](https://github.com/hiyenwong/ai_collection/tree/HEAD/collection/skills/neuroscience/multi-scale-info-geometry-neural) · ★4 · 3 copies)
- **multi-scale-information-geometry-neural**: Multi-scale information geometry framework for analyzing neural population codes. Extends Fisher information metric across stimulus coarse-graining scales to reveal mutual information structure. Use when analyzing: (1) neural population coding geometry, (2) Fisher information limitations in neural data, (3) representational geometry from first principles, (4) mutual information estimation from neural responses, (5) diffusion model-based neural encoding analysis. Trigger: multi-scale Fisher, information geometry neural, representational geometry, neural population code, Fisher information metr… ([hiyenwong/ai_collection](https://github.com/hiyenwong/ai_collection/tree/HEAD/collection/skills/neuroscience/multi-scale-information-geometry-neural) · ★4 · 3 copies)
- **multi-scale-sliding-window-binning**: Use when analyzing Seq-Scope/Slide-seq DGEs with spatial coordinates where simple square-grid aggregation is too coarse to resolve cell-type boundaries and finer, overlapping-bin clustering is desired. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/multi-scale-sliding-window-binning))
